   real heading; obstacles are axis-aligned. */
function rectHit(pcx, pcz, theta, ocx, ocz, ohw, ohl) {
  // The player's box is its real rotated rectangle, but it SHRINKS while the
  // car is angled (matching the real game: an angling car presents a smaller
  // hitbox, which is what makes threading a lane change possible at 30°).
  const shrink = 1 - 0.5 * Math.min(1, Math.abs(theta) / 0.35);
  const hw = HIT_HW * shrink, hl = HIT_HL * shrink;
  const c = Math.cos(theta), s = Math.sin(theta);
  const P = [
    [pcx + hw * c - hl * s, pcz + hw * s + hl * c],
    [pcx - hw * c - hl * s, pcz - hw * s + hl * c],
    [pcx - hw * c + hl * s, pcz - hw * s - hl * c],
    [pcx + hw * c + hl * s, pcz + hw * s - hl * c],
  ];
  const O = [
    [ocx + ohw, ocz + ohl], [ocx - ohw, ocz + ohl],
    [ocx - ohw, ocz - ohl], [ocx + ohw, ocz - ohl],
  ];
  const axes = [[1, 0], [0, 1], [c, s], [-s, c]];
  for (const ax of axes) {
    let pMin = 1e18, pMax = -1e18, oMin = 1e18, oMax = -1e18;
    for (const q of P) { const d = q[0] * ax[0] + q[1] * ax[1]; pMin = Math.min(pMin, d); pMax = Math.max(pMax, d); }
    for (const q of O) { const d = q[0] * ax[0] + q[1] * ax[1]; oMin = Math.min(oMin, d); oMax = Math.max(oMax, d); }
    if (pMax < oMin || oMax < pMin) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
const FONT = "'Luckiest Guy', 'Arial Black', sans-serif";

// road layout in lane units: rainbow shoulder [-1.55,-0.55], lanes 0..3,
// green cashout boundary at 2.5, road edges -1.6 and 3.5
function proj(z) { return 1 / (1 + z * 0.006); }
function screenY(z) { return PLAYER_SCREEN_Y - 14.5 * z / (1 + 0.004 * z); }
function screenX(lat, p) { return W / 2 + (lat - 1.5) * LANE_PX * p; }

/* ============================== GAME ============================== */
const game = {
  state: "idle",                 // idle | running | dead | cashed
  lane: 1,
  x: 1,
  theta: 0,                      // heading (rad); the sprite rotation IS the physics state
  cars: [],                      // {lane, z, f (forward-speed fraction), col}
  barriers: [],                  // {boundary (0..2), z0, z1}
  speed: 15,
  runT: 0,
  density: 0.55,
  lastGain: null,
  dist: 0,
  score: 0,
  deaths: 0,
  lastOpen: 1,
  waveGap: 26,
  zSinceWave: 0,
  stateAt: 0,
  cashTimer: 0,
  banked: 0,
  bankedTotal: 0,
  tilt: 0,
  particles: [],
};

const CAR_COLORS = [
  { body: "#e8e8e8", dark: "#b9b9bd" },  // white
  { body: "#e9c832", dark: "#bb9c1e" },  // yellow
  { body: "#d1483c", dark: "#a3352c" },  // red
  { body: "#e8e8e8", dark: "#b9b9bd" },
];

function multiplier() { return Math.min(2.5, 1 + game.score * 0.004); }

function resetRun() {
  game.cars = []; game.barriers = []; game.particles = [];
  game.lane = 1; game.x = 1; game.theta = 0;
  game.lastOpen = 1;
  game.speed = 15; game.waveGap = 34;
  game.runT = 0; game.density = 0.55;
  game.zSinceWave = game.waveGap;
  game.dist = 0; game.score = 0;
  game.cashTimer = 0;
  game.lastGain = null;
  game.state = "running";
}

function spawnWave() {
  // The open traffic lane walks by at most one step, so a correct dodge is
  // always reachable. Lane 3 (cashout) gets its own occasional traffic.
  const r = rng();
  let open = game.lastOpen;
  if (r < 0.38) open = Math.max(0, open - 1);
  else if (r < 0.76) open = Math.min(2, open + 1);
  const needCross = open !== game.lastOpen ? Math.min(open, game.lastOpen) : -1;
  game.lastOpen = open;

  // each wave drives at its own forward speed (fraction of the player's), with
  // slight per-car variation; the player is much faster, so passes are quick
  const fWave = 0.12 + rng() * 0.12;
  const mkCar = lane => ({
    lane, z: SPAWN_Z + (rng() - 0.5) * 3,
    f: Math.max(0.08, Math.min(0.3, fWave + (rng() - 0.5) * 0.04)),
    col: (rng() * CAR_COLORS.length) | 0, threatAt: 0, rtLogged: false, passed: false,
  });
  for (let l = 0; l < 3; l++) {
    if (l === open) continue;
    if (rng() < game.density) game.cars.push(mkCar(l));
  }
  if (rng() < 0.25) game.cars.push(mkCar(CASH_LANE));

  // Barrier trap: red blocks on a lane boundary, never on the boundary the
  // guaranteed path needs to cross for this wave.
  if (rng() < 0.34) {
    const options = [0, 1, 2].filter(b => b !== needCross);
    const b = options[(rng() * options.length) | 0];
    const len = 5 + rng() * 4;
    game.barriers.push({ boundary: b, z0: SPAWN_Z - game.waveGap * 0.55, z1: SPAWN_Z - game.waveGap * 0.55 + len });
  }
}

function barrierBlocks(fromLane, toLane, zLead) {
  const b = Math.min(fromLane, toLane);
  for (const bar of game.barriers) {
    if (bar.boundary !== b) continue;
    if (bar.z1 > -HLZ && bar.z0 < zLead) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function laneChange(dir, swipe) {
  if (game.state !== "running") return;
  const from = game.lane;
  const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(LANES - 1, from + dir));
  if (to === from) return;
  game.lane = to;
  // Reaction time: nearest un-logged threat car that was in the lane we left.
  let best = null;
  for (const car of game.cars) {
    if (car.passed || car.rtLogged || !car.threatAt) continue;
    if (car.lane !== from) continue;
    if (!best || car.z < best.z) best = car;
  }
  if (best) {
    best.rtLogged = true;
    const rt = performance.now() - best.threatAt;
    AC.recordDodge(rt, Math.max(0.01, (best.z - COLL_Z) / closing(best)));
  }
  // contested move: entering a lane with a car close enough to matter
  for (const car of game.cars) {
    if (car.passed || car.lane !== to) continue;
    if (car.z > -COLL_Z && (car.z - COLL_Z) / closing(car) < 1.0) { AC.recordRisk(); break; }
  }
  AC.moves++;
  if (swipe) AC.recordSwipe(swipe);
}

const puffRand = mulberry32(0xf1a2);
function emitPuff(kind) {
  const r = puffRand;
  const px = screenX(game.x, 1), py = PLAYER_SCREEN_Y - 42;
  if (kind === "smoke") {
    game.particles.push({
      x: px + (r() - 0.5) * 56, y: py - 24 - r() * 40,
      vx: (r() - 0.5) * 14, vy: -48 - r() * 50,
      r: 15 + r() * 16, grow: 9, age: 0, life: 1.9 + r() * 1.1, kind,
    });
  } else if (kind === "ember") {
    game.particles.push({
      x: px + (r() - 0.5) * 48, y: py - 6 - r() * 26,
      vx: (r() - 0.5) * 12, vy: -36 - r() * 36,
      r: 12 + r() * 12, grow: 6, age: 0, life: 1.3 + r() * 0.8, kind,
    });
  } else {
    game.particles.push({
      x: px + (r() - 0.5) * 34, y: py + 8 - r() * 20,
      vx: (r() - 0.5) * 10, vy: -22 - r() * 30,
      r: 10 + r() * 12, grow: 4, age: 0, life: 0.7 + r() * 0.5, kind: "fire",
    });
  }
}

function crash() {
  game.state = "dead";
  game.stateAt = performance.now();
  game.deaths++;
  game.emitAcc = 0;
  AC.recordDeath();
  AC.recordRunEnd("crash");
  for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) emitPuff("smoke");
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) emitPuff("ember");
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) emitPuff("fire");
}

let lastFrame = performance.now();
function frame(now) {
  const dt = Math.min((now - lastFrame) / 1000, 0.05);
  lastFrame = now;

  if (game.state === "running") {
    // difficulty ramps continuously with distance: the road speeds up and the
    // waves pack tighter, so the reaction window shrinks the longer you survive
    game.runT += dt;
    game.speed = Math.min(34, 15 + game.runT * 0.42);
    game.waveGap = Math.max(19, 34 - game.runT * 0.30);
    game.density = Math.min(0.9, 0.55 + game.runT * 0.007);

    const dz = game.speed * dt;
    game.dist += dz;
    game.zSinceWave += dz;
    if (game.zSinceWave >= game.waveGap) {
      game.zSinceWave = 0;
      spawnWave();
    }

    // steering: the swipe sets a target lane; the car banks to ~30° and its
    // lateral velocity comes from the heading — it travels the angled path
    const dxLane = game.lane - game.x;
    let steerTarget = 0;
    if (Math.abs(dxLane) > 0.012) {
      steerTarget = Math.sign(dxLane) * MAX_STEER * Math.min(1, Math.abs(dxLane) / 0.22);
    }
    game.theta += (steerTarget - game.theta) * Math.min(1, dt * STEER_RATE);
    const latSpeed = Math.tan(game.theta) * (game.speed * ZPX) / LANE_PX; // lanes/s
    game.x += latSpeed * dt;
    if (Math.abs(game.lane - game.x) < 0.04 && Math.abs(game.theta) < 0.1) {
      game.x = game.lane;
      game.theta = 0;
    }
    const pcx = game.x * LANE_PX;

    // traffic drives forward at its own speed; a faster follower matches the
    // car ahead of it instead of rear-ending it
    for (const a of game.cars) {
      for (const b of game.cars) {
        if (a === b || a.lane !== b.lane || a.passed || b.passed) continue;
        if (b.z > a.z && b.z - a.z < COLL_Z + 1.5 && b.f < a.f) b.f = a.f;
      }
    }
    for (const car of game.cars) {
      const cl = closing(car);
      car.z -= cl * dt;
      if (!car.passed && car.lane === game.lane && !car.threatAt) {
        const tti = (car.z - COLL_Z) / cl;
        if (tti > 0 && tti < THREAT_WINDOW) car.threatAt = performance.now();
      }
      if (!car.passed && car.z < -COLL_Z) {
        car.passed = true;
        // score is multiplied by the lane you were in when you passed
        const mult = LANE_MULT[game.lane];
        game.score += mult;
        if (mult) game.lastGain = { mult, at: now, y: PLAYER_SCREEN_Y - 90 };
        AC.rowsPassed++;
      }
      if (!car.passed && Math.abs(car.z) < COLL_Z + 2 &&
          rectHit(pcx, 0, game.theta, car.lane * LANE_PX, car.z * ZPX, HIT_HW, HIT_HL)) {
        crash();
      }
    }
    for (const bar of game.barriers) {
      bar.z0 -= dz; bar.z1 -= dz; // rails are parked on the road: full closing speed
      if (bar.z1 > -HLZ - 1 && bar.z0 < HLZ + 1 &&
          rectHit(pcx, 0, game.theta,
                  (bar.boundary + 0.5) * LANE_PX, ((bar.z0 + bar.z1) / 2) * ZPX,
                  4, ((bar.z1 - bar.z0) / 2) * ZPX)) {
        crash();
      }
    }
    game.cars = game.cars.filter(c => c.z > -10);
    game.barriers = game.barriers.filter(b => b.z1 > -10);

    // cashout: hold the lane
    if (game.lane === CASH_LANE && Math.abs(game.x - CASH_LANE) < 0.1) {
      game.cashTimer += dt;
      if (game.cashTimer >= CASH_HOLD) {
        game.banked = ENTRY_FEE * multiplier();
        game.bankedTotal += game.banked;
        game.state = "cashed";
        game.stateAt = now;
        AC.recordRunEnd("cashout");
      }
    } else {
      game.cashTimer = Math.max(0, game.cashTimer - dt * 2);
    }
  }

  // crash-scene tilt eases in while dead; the plume keeps burning
  const targetTilt = game.state === "dead" ? -0.17 : 0;
  game.tilt += (targetTilt - game.tilt) * Math.min(1, dt * 4);
  if (game.state === "dead") {
    game.emitAcc = (game.emitAcc || 0) + dt;
    while (game.emitAcc > 0.11) {
      game.emitAcc -= 0.11;
      emitPuff("smoke");
      if (puffRand() < 0.5) emitPuff("ember");
      if (puffRand() < 0.9) emitPuff("fire");
    }
  }

  // bots continue past end screens on their own; humans tap
  if ((game.state === "dead" || game.state === "cashed") && Bot.mode && now - game.stateAt > 900) {
    resetRun();
  }

  AC.tickRisks(now);
  draw(now, dt);
  Bot.tick(now);
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}

/* ============================== DRAW ============================== */
function otext(txt, x, y, size, fill, stroke, strokeW, align) {
  ctx.font = size + "px " + FONT;
  ctx.textAlign = align || "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.lineJoin = "round";
  if (stroke) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = stroke;
    ctx.lineWidth = strokeW || Math.max(3, size * 0.14);
    ctx.strokeText(txt, x, y);
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = fill;
  ctx.fillText(txt, x, y);
}

function draw(now, dt) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // crash tilt rotates the whole world
  if (Math.abs(game.tilt) > 0.002) {
    ctx.translate(W / 2, H * 0.55);
    ctx.rotate(game.tilt);
    ctx.scale(1.12, 1.12);
    ctx.translate(-W / 2, -H * 0.55);
  }

  drawGround();
  drawRoad();
  drawCashoutText();
  drawRainbow();

  // far-to-near
  const items = [];
  for (const c of game.cars) if (c.z > -6 && c.z < SPAWN_Z + 2) items.push({ z: c.z, t: "car", o: c });
  for (const b of game.barriers) if (b.z1 > -6 && b.z0 < SPAWN_Z + 2) items.push({ z: b.z1, t: "bar", o: b });
  items.sort((a, b2) => b2.z - a.z);
  for (const it of items) it.t === "car" ? drawTraffic(it.o) : drawBarrier(it.o);

  drawPlayer();
  if (document.getElementById("showHitbox").checked) drawHitboxes();
  drawParticles(dt);
  ctx.restore();

  // dim overlays for idle / dead states
  if (game.state === "idle") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,14,0.52)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    drawStartScreen();
  } else if (game.state === "dead") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,14,0.35)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    otext("CRASHED", W / 2, 180, 72, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 12);
    otext("TAP TO CONTINUE", W / 2, H - 70, 36, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 8);
  } else if (game.state === "cashed") {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,14,0.35)";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    otext("CASHED OUT", W / 2, 180, 58, "#3ede6a", "#123a1c", 10);
    otext("$" + game.banked.toFixed(2), W / 2, 250, 54, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 10);
    otext("TAP TO CONTINUE", W / 2, H - 70, 36, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 8);
  } else {
    drawHUD();
  }

  if (Bot.mode) {
    ctx.font = "13px ui-monospace, monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#f5b942";
    ctx.fillText("BOT: " + Bot.mode, 14, H - 16);
  }

  // phone notch
  ctx.fillStyle = "#000";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(W / 2 - 72, 16, 144, 34, 17);
  ctx.fill();
}

function drawGround() {
  ctx.fillStyle = "#c9bc9e";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  // scrolling mosaic patches
  const period = 5;
  const off = game.dist % period;
  for (let zi = -1; zi < 13; zi++) {
    const z = zi * period - off;
    const zRow = Math.floor((z + game.dist) / period + 0.5);
    const r = mulberry32(zRow * 7919);
    const y1 = screenY(Math.max(-3, z)), y2 = screenY(z + period);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const left = r() < 0.5;
      const p = proj(Math.max(0, z));
      const edge = left ? screenX(-1.6, p) : screenX(3.5, p);
      const gw = 14 + r() * 42;
      const gx = left ? edge - 12 - r() * 120 - gw : edge + 12 + r() * 120;
      ctx.fillStyle = r() < 0.5 ? "rgba(0,0,0,0.06)" : "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)";
      ctx.fillRect(gx, Math.min(y1, y2), gw, Math.abs(y1 - y2) * (0.4 + r() * 0.6));
    }
  }
}

function quad(latA, latB, z1, z2, fill) {
  const pa = proj(Math.max(0, z1)), pb = proj(Math.max(0, z2));
  const ya = screenY(z1), yb = screenY(z2);
  ctx.fillStyle = fill;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(screenX(latA, pa), ya);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(latA, pb), yb);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(latB, pb), yb);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(latB, pa), ya);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
}

function drawRoad() {
  const Z0 = -14, Z1 = SPAWN_Z + 6;
  // curbs
  quad(-0.68, -0.5, Z0, Z1, "#4a463f");
  quad(3.5, 3.68, Z0, Z1, "#54524e");
  // asphalt: lane 0 (rainbow) through the cashout lane
  quad(-0.5, 3.5, Z0, Z1, "#6d6f73");
  // solid white edge lines
  quad(-0.5, -0.44, Z0, Z1, "#e8e8e8");
  quad(3.44, 3.5, Z0, Z1, "#e8e8e8");
  // green cashout boundary
  quad(2.47, 2.53, Z0, Z1, "#2ed94f");
  // dashed separators: 0|1 and 1|2
  const dashLen = 2.6, gap = 4.6, period = dashLen + gap;
  const off = game.dist % period;
  for (const b of [0.5, 1.5]) {
    for (let zi = -3; zi < 10; zi++) {
      const z = zi * period - off;
      const za = Math.max(-14, z), zb = z + dashLen;
      if (zb < -14) continue;
      quad(b - 0.026, b + 0.026, za, zb, "#f2f2f2");
    }
  }
}

function drawCashoutText() {
  const period = 26;
  const off = game.dist % period;
  ctx.fillStyle = "#27e04c";
  for (let zi = 0; zi < 3; zi++) {
    const z = zi * period - off + 10;
    if (z < 1) continue;
    const p = proj(z);
    const x = screenX(CASH_LANE, p), y = screenY(z);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);
    ctx.font = 46 * p + "px " + FONT;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.92;
    ctx.fillText("CASHOUT", 0, 14 * p);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

function drawRainbow() {
  // scrolling hue ramp on the left shoulder, blurred soft like the real thing
  const zTop = SPAWN_Z + 6, zBot = -14;
  const yBot = screenY(zBot), yTop = screenY(zTop);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, yBot, 0, yTop);
  const stops = 26;
  for (let k = 0; k <= stops; k++) {
    const z = zBot + (zTop - zBot) * (k / stops);
    const hue = (((z + game.dist) * 5.6) % 360 + 360) % 360;
    g.addColorStop(k / stops, "hsl(" + hue + ",94%,53%)");
  }
  const pa = proj(0), pb = proj(zTop);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.filter = "blur(7px)";
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.62;
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(screenX(-0.46, pa), yBot);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(-0.46, pb), yTop);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(0.46, pb), yTop);
  ctx.lineTo(screenX(0.46, pa), yBot);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* Cartoon car, seen from above-behind: hood at top, rear (plate) at bottom. */
function drawCarBody(x, y, p, col, opts) {
  const w = 58 * p, h = 108 * p;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  if (opts && opts.rot) ctx.rotate(opts.rot);
  // shadow
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.25)";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.ellipse(2 * p, 4 * p, w * 0.58, h * 0.52, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  // body
  ctx.fillStyle = col.body;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 10 * p);
  ctx.fill();
  // hood shading + vents
  ctx.fillStyle = col.dark;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(-w / 2 + 4 * p, -h / 2 + 3 * p, w - 8 * p, h * 0.16, 6 * p);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.5)";
  for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++)
    ctx.fillRect(i * 9 * p - 4 * p, -h / 2 + 8 * p, 8 * p, 2 * p);
  // windshield
  ctx.fillStyle = "#20242c";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(-w / 2 + 5 * p, -h * 0.24);
  ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 5 * p, -h * 0.24);
  ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 8 * p, -h * 0.02);
  ctx.lineTo(-w / 2 + 8 * p, -h * 0.02);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  // side mirrors
  ctx.fillStyle = col.body;
  ctx.fillRect(-w / 2 - 5 * p, -h * 0.22, 6 * p, 5 * p);
  ctx.fillRect(w / 2 - 1 * p, -h * 0.22, 6 * p, 5 * p);
  // roof band
  // rear window
  ctx.fillStyle = "#20242c";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(-w / 2 + 8 * p, h * 0.16);
  ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 8 * p, h * 0.16);
  ctx.lineTo(w / 2 - 5 * p, h * 0.34);
  ctx.lineTo(-w / 2 + 5 * p, h * 0.34);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fill();
  // taillights
  ctx.fillStyle = "#f2921e";
  ctx.fillRect(-w / 2 + 3 * p, h / 2 - 8 * p, 9 * p, 5 * p);
  ctx.fillRect(w / 2 - 12 * p, h / 2 - 8 * p, 9 * p, 5 * p);
  // license plate
  if (opts && opts.plate) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#f4f2e8";
    const pw = 22 * p, ph = 8 * p;
    ctx.fillRect(-pw / 2, h / 2 - 9.5 * p, pw, ph);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#333";
    ctx.font = 6 * p + "px Georgia, serif";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.fillText("GBE-86", 0, h / 2 - 3 * p);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawTraffic(c) {
  const p = proj(Math.max(0, c.z));
  drawCarBody(screenX(c.lane, p), screenY(c.z), p, CAR_COLORS[c.col || 0], {});
}

function drawPlayer() {
  // the sprite rotation is the actual physics heading
  const rot = game.state === "dead" ? 0.5 : game.theta;
  drawCarBody(screenX(game.x, 1), PLAYER_SCREEN_Y - 30, 1,
    { body: "#2f8be6", dark: "#2570bd" }, { plate: true, rot });
}

function drawBarrier(b) {
  // discrete red blocks along the boundary
  const lat = b.boundary + 0.5;
  const blockLen = 1.6, gap = 2.4;
  for (let z = b.z0; z < b.z1; z += blockLen + gap) {
    const za = Math.max(0.1, z), zb = Math.min(b.z1, z + blockLen);
    if (zb <= za) continue;
    const pa = proj(za), pb = proj(zb);
    const ya = screenY(za), yb = screenY(zb);
    const hw = 9;
    const lift = 26; // block height in px
    // side face
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7e1410";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(screenX(lat, pa) - hw * pa, ya);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pb) - hw * pb, yb);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pb) + hw * pb, yb);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pa) + hw * pa, ya);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    // top face
    ctx.fillStyle = "#c0201a";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(screenX(lat, pa) - hw * pa, ya - lift * pa);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pb) - hw * pb, yb - lift * pb);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pb) + hw * pb, yb - lift * pb);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pa) + hw * pa, ya - lift * pa);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    // front face
    ctx.fillStyle = "#9c1a15";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(screenX(lat, pa) - hw * pa, ya - lift * pa);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pa) + hw * pa, ya - lift * pa);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pa) + hw * pa, ya);
    ctx.lineTo(screenX(lat, pa) - hw * pa, ya);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

/* Draws the ACTUAL collision geometry the physics uses (same math as rectHit),
   so the shrinking rotated player box is visible while cornering. */
function drawHitboxes() {
  const toS = (cxPx, czZ) => ({ x: W / 2 + (cxPx / LANE_PX - 1.5) * LANE_PX, y: screenY(czZ) });
  // player: rotated + shrunk exactly as the SAT test sees it
  const th = game.theta;
  const shrink = 1 - 0.5 * Math.min(1, Math.abs(th) / 0.35);
  const hw = HIT_HW * shrink, hl = HIT_HL * shrink;
  const c = Math.cos(th), s = Math.sin(th);
  const pc = toS(game.x * LANE_PX, 0);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#3ecf8e";
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.setLineDash([5, 4]);
  ctx.beginPath();
  const corners = [[hw, hl], [-hw, hl], [-hw, -hl], [hw, -hl]];
  corners.forEach((q, i) => {
    const x = pc.x + q[0] * c - q[1] * s;
    const y = pc.y - (q[0] * s + q[1] * c);
    i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y);
  });
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#3ecf8e";
  ctx.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.fillText((shrink * 100).toFixed(0) + "% · " + (th * 57.3).toFixed(0) + "°", pc.x, pc.y + hl + 14);
  // obstacles (axis-aligned in world space)
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#f4544c";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  for (const car of game.cars) {
    if (car.passed || car.z > SPAWN_Z || car.z < -8) continue;
    const a = toS(car.lane * LANE_PX, car.z + HLZ), b = toS(car.lane * LANE_PX, car.z - HLZ);
    ctx.strokeRect(a.x - HIT_HW, a.y, HIT_HW * 2, b.y - a.y);
  }
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#f5b942";
  for (const bar of game.barriers) {
    if (bar.z1 < -8 || bar.z0 > SPAWN_Z) continue;
    const a = toS((bar.boundary + 0.5) * LANE_PX, bar.z1), b = toS((bar.boundary + 0.5) * LANE_PX, bar.z0);
    ctx.strokeRect(a.x - 4, a.y, 8, b.y - a.y);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawParticles(dt) {
  for (const p of game.particles) {
    p.age += dt;
    if (p.age < 0) continue;
    p.x += p.vx * dt;
    p.y += p.vy * dt;
    p.r += p.grow * dt;
    const u = p.age / p.life;
    if (u >= 1) continue;
    if (p.kind === "smoke") {
      const shade = Math.round(40 + u * 26);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(" + (shade + 12) + "," + shade + "," + shade + "," + (0.5 * (1 - u * 0.75)).toFixed(2) + ")";
    } else if (p.kind === "ember") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(118,36,20," + (0.6 * (1 - u)).toFixed(2) + ")";
    } else {
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x, p.y, 1, p.x, p.y, p.r);
      g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,236,120," + (0.95 * (1 - u)) + ")");
      g.addColorStop(0.5, "rgba(248,142,30," + (0.9 * (1 - u)) + ")");
      g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(180,50,10,0)");
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
    }
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  game.particles = game.particles.filter(p => p.age < p.life);
}

function drawHUD() {
  otext("$" + Math.round(game.bankedTotal), W / 2, 150, 66, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 12);
  otext("SCORE: " + game.score, W / 2, 192, 26, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 6);
  const m = Math.round(multiplier() * 2) / 2;
  otext((m % 1 === 0 ? m : m.toFixed(1)) + "X", W / 2, 250, 54, "#3c3f45", "#ffffff", 8);
  // which lane you're in = how fast score accrues
  const lm = LANE_MULT[game.lane];
  otext(lm ? "LANE " + lm + "X" : "CASHOUT LANE", W / 2, 300, 22,
    lm === 5 ? "#ff5ce1" : lm === 2 ? "#7ec9ff" : lm === 1 ? "#d7dde8" : "#3ede6a", "#1c1c1c", 5);
  // floating +N on each pass
  if (game.lastGain) {
    const age = (performance.now() - game.lastGain.at) / 700;
    if (age < 1) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - age;
      otext("+" + game.lastGain.mult, screenX(game.x, 1) + 46,
        game.lastGain.y - age * 40, 30,
        game.lastGain.mult === 5 ? "#ff5ce1" : "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 6);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    } else game.lastGain = null;
  }
  if (game.cashTimer > 0) {
    const frac = Math.min(1, game.cashTimer / CASH_HOLD);
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20,60,30,0.55)";
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(W / 2 - 74, 268, 148, 16, 8); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "#2ed94f";
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(W / 2 - 74, 268, 148 * frac, 16, 8); ctx.fill();
    otext("CASHING OUT", W / 2, 281, 13, "#0b2010", null, 0);
  }
}

function drawStartScreen() {
  // settings row (decorative, matching the real start screen)
  const icons = [
    { x: W * 0.18, label: "SOUND" },
    { x: W * 0.5, label: "MUSIC" },
    { x: W * 0.82, label: "HAPTICS" },
  ];
  for (const ic of icons) {
    drawIcon(ic.label, ic.x, 118);
    otext(ic.label, ic.x, 172, 17, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 5);
  }
  // swipe arrows
  drawArrows(W / 2, 262);
  otext("SWIPE LEFT/RIGHT", W / 2, 334, 30, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 7);
  otext("TO CHANGE LANES", W / 2, 366, 30, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 7);
  // fork icon
  drawFork(W / 2, 432);
  otext("USE CASHOUT LANE", W / 2, 508, 30, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 7);
  otext("TO KEEP YOUR SCORE", W / 2, 540, 30, "#ffffff", "#1c1c1c", 7);
  otext("TAP TO START", W / 2, 806, 52, "#f2e42a", "#1c1c1c", 11);
}

function drawIcon(kind, x, y) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#f2e42a";
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#f2e42a";
  ctx.lineWidth = 4;
  ctx.lineCap = "round";
  if (kind === "SOUND") {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-16, -7); ctx.lineTo(-7, -7); ctx.lineTo(4, -17); ctx.lineTo(4, 17); ctx.lineTo(-7, 7); ctx.lineTo(-16, 7);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(6, 0, 12, -0.9, 0.9); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(6, 0, 19, -0.9, 0.9); ctx.stroke();
  } else if (kind === "MUSIC") {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-6, 12, 7, 5, -0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillRect(0, -16, 4, 28);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, -16); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(12, -14, 16, -6);
    ctx.lineTo(16, -12); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(10, -20, 0, -22);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  } else {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-8, -16, 16, 32, 5); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-14, 0, 9, 1.8, 4.5); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(14, 0, 9, -1.35, 1.35); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawArrows(x, y) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#b7b7b7";
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#3a3a3a";
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  const arrow = (dir, dy) => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-34 * dir, dy);
    ctx.lineTo(6 * dir, dy);
    ctx.lineTo(6 * dir, dy - 10);
    ctx.lineTo(34 * dir, dy + 5);
    ctx.lineTo(6 * dir, dy + 20);
    ctx.lineTo(6 * dir, dy + 10);
    ctx.lineTo(-34 * dir, dy + 10);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  };
  arrow(-1, -22);
  arrow(1, 8);
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawFork(x, y) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#b7b7b7";
  ctx.lineWidth = 11;
  ctx.lineCap = "round";
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-14, 34); ctx.lineTo(-14, -28); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(12, 34); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(12, 0, 30, -22); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = "#b7b7b7";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(38, -34); ctx.lineTo(16, -28); ctx.lineTo(30, -12);
  ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ========================= INPUT PIPELINE =========================
   Humans and bots feed the exact same pipeline. The detector only ever
   sees what comes out of it. */
const Input = {
  active: null,
  begin(x, y, trusted, source) {
    this.active = { points: [{ x, y, t: performance.now() }], trusted, source, applied: false };
  },
  move(x, y) {
    const s = this.active;
    if (!s) return;
    s.points.push({ x, y, t: performance.now() });
    if (!s.applied) {
      const dx = x - s.points[0].x;
      if (Math.abs(dx) >= SWIPE_THRESHOLD) {
        s.applied = true;
        laneChange(dx > 0 ? 1 : -1, null);
      }
    }
  },
  end() {
    const s = this.active;
    this.active = null;
    if (!s) return;
    if (!s.applied) {
      const d = Math.hypot(
        s.points[s.points.length - 1].x - s.points[0].x,
        s.points[s.points.length - 1].y - s.points[0].y);
      if (d > 3 || s.points.length <= 3) AC.recordAbort();
      return;
    }
    AC.recordSwipe({
      points: s.points,
      trusted: s.trusted,
      source: s.source,
      dur: s.points[s.points.length - 1].t - s.points[0].t,
    });
  },
};

function advanceState() {
  // tap-to-start / tap-to-continue
  if (game.state === "idle") { resetRun(); return true; }
  if ((game.state === "dead" || game.state === "cashed") && performance.now() - game.stateAt > 450) {
    resetRun(); return true;
  }
  return game.state !== "running";
}

cv.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
  if (Bot.mode) return;
  if (advanceState()) return;
  cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  Input.begin(e.offsetX, e.offsetY, e.isTrusted, "pointer");
});
cv.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (Bot.mode) return;
  if (Input.active) Input.move(e.offsetX, e.offsetY);
});
cv.addEventListener("pointerup", () => { if (!Bot.mode) Input.end(); });
cv.addEventListener("pointercancel", () => { if (!Bot.mode) Input.end(); });

window.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (Bot.mode) return;
  if (e.key !== "ArrowLeft" && e.key !== "ArrowRight") return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if (advanceState()) return;
  laneChange(e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1, null);
  AC.recordKey(e.isTrusted);
});

/* ============================== BOT ==============================
   Three attack modes, all driven through the same Input pipeline:
   - perfect:    instant reaction, machine-clean synthetic swipes.
   - mirror:     replay of a small corpus of "recorded human" traces,
                 perturbed per use, with sampled human-ish reaction times.
   - generative: a fresh random human-shaped trace per swipe with injected
                 noise. Built to beat replay detection and jitter checks. */
const Bot = {
  mode: null,
  rand: mulberry32(99),
  pending: null,
  swipeRun: null,
  baseTraces: [],

  buildCorpus() {
    const mk = seed => {
      const r = mulberry32(seed);
      const n = 14, dur = 150 + r() * 70, len = 115 + r() * 30, arc = (r() - 0.5) * 18;
      const skew = 0.38 + r() * 0.18;
      const pts = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const u = i / (n - 1);
        const p = u < skew ? (u / skew) * (u / skew) * 0.5
                           : 0.5 + (1 - Math.pow(1 - (u - skew) / (1 - skew), 2)) * 0.5;
        pts.push({
          x: p * len + (r() - 0.5) * 2.6,
          y: Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * arc + (r() - 0.5) * 2.2,
          t: u * dur,
        });
      }
      return pts;
    };
    this.baseTraces = [mk(11), mk(23), mk(37), mk(53)];
  },

  set(mode) {
    this.mode = mode;
    this.pending = null;
    this.swipeRun = null;
    Input.active = null;
    if (mode && game.state !== "running") resetRun();
    document.getElementById("btnHuman").classList.toggle("active", !mode);
    document.getElementById("btnPerfect").classList.toggle("active", mode === "perfect");
    document.getElementById("btnMirror").classList.toggle("active", mode === "mirror");
    document.getElementById("btnGen").classList.toggle("active", mode === "generative");
  },

  laneClear(l, threatCar) {
    if (barrierBlocks(game.lane, l, game.speed * 0.9)) return false;
    // a rail on EITHER boundary of the destination lane can graze the angled
    // tail during the settle — treat the lane as unsafe while one is alongside
    for (const bar of game.barriers) {
      if (bar.boundary !== l - 1 && bar.boundary !== l) continue;
      if (bar.z1 > -HLZ - 3 && bar.z0 < HLZ + game.speed * 0.6) return false;
    }
    for (const car of game.cars) {
      if (car === threatCar || car.passed || car.lane !== l) continue;
      const lead = closing(car) * 0.55 + COLL_Z;
      if (car.z > -COLL_Z - 1 && car.z < lead) return false;
    }
    return true;
  },

  // seconds until this lane's nearest live car reaches the player
  firstObs(l) {
    let m = 99;
    for (const car of game.cars) {
      if (car.passed || car.lane !== l || car.z <= -COLL_Z) continue;
      const t = Math.max(0, (car.z - COLL_Z) / closing(car));
      if (t < m) m = t;
    }
    return m;
  },

  crossOk(from, dir) {
    return !barrierBlocks(from, from + dir, game.speed * 0.9);
  },

  /* Utility-based planning: each lane scores by time-to-first-collision, with
     penalties for the cashout lane and travel distance; a lane only counts if
     every crossing on the way is barrier-free and every intermediate lane can
     be passed through right now. Repositions proactively toward open road
     instead of waiting to be boxed in. */
  safeDir() {
    const here = game.lane;
    const tHere = this.firstObs(here);
    const clamp = t => Math.min(t, 4);
    // survival overrides preference: when a hit is imminent, the cashout-lane
    // penalty vanishes and any better lane wins
    const dying = tHere < 0.7;
    // value = safety + greed: the 5x lane is worth real risk, the cashout lane
    // scores nothing so it is avoided unless banking. Survival overrides both.
    const val = l => dying ? 0 : LANE_MULT[l] * 0.30;
    const margin = dying ? 0.05 : 0.6;
    let best = here, bestU = clamp(tHere) + val(here);
    for (let l = 0; l < LANES; l++) {
      if (l === here) continue;
      const dir = Math.sign(l - here);
      let ok = true;
      for (let m = here; m !== l; m += dir) {
        if (!this.crossOk(m, dir)) { ok = false; break; }
        const step = m + dir;
        if (step !== l && this.firstObs(step) < 0.9) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if (!ok || !this.laneClear(l, null)) continue;
      const u = clamp(this.firstObs(l)) + val(l) - 0.35 * Math.abs(l - here);
      if (u > bestU + margin) { best = l; bestU = u; }
    }
    if (best === here) return 0;
    const urgent = tHere < (this.mode === "perfect" ? 0.95 : THREAT_WINDOW);
    if (!urgent) {
      if (game.x !== game.lane) return 0;                        // settle first
      if (tHere > 2.2 && bestU - clamp(tHere) < 1.0) return 0;   // reposition only for real gains
    }
    return Math.sign(best - here);
  },

  tick(now) {
    if (!this.mode || game.state !== "running") {
      if (game.state !== "running") { this.pending = null; this.swipeRun = null; }
      return;
    }

    if (this.swipeRun) {
      const s = this.swipeRun;
      const el = now - s.t0;
      while (s.i < s.trace.length && s.trace[s.i].t <= el) {
        const p = s.trace[s.i++];
        Input.move(s.startX + p.x * s.dir, s.startY + p.y);
      }
      if (s.i >= s.trace.length) { this.swipeRun = null; Input.end(); }
      return;
    }

    if (this.pending) {
      if (now >= this.pending.fireAt) {
        this.pending = null;
        const dir = this.safeDir(); // re-validate at fire time
        if (dir) this.startSwipe(dir, now);
      }
      return;
    }

    const dir = this.safeDir();
    if (!dir) return;

    if (this.mode === "perfect") {
      this.startSwipe(dir, now);
    } else {
      // mirror + generative both sample a clean Gaussian RT — no lapse tail
      const rt = Math.max(150, gauss(this.rand, 235, 42));
      this.pending = { fireAt: now + rt, dir };
    }
  },

  startSwipe(dir, now) {
    const trusted = document.getElementById("hwInject").checked;
    const startX = screenX(game.x, 1) + (this.rand() - 0.5) * 10;
    const startY = 560 + (this.rand() - 0.5) * 24;
    Input.begin(startX, startY, trusted, "bot");
    let trace;
    if (this.mode === "perfect") {
      trace = [];
      for (let i = 1; i <= 8; i++) trace.push({ x: (i / 8) * 110, y: 0, t: (i / 8) * 90 });
    } else if (this.mode === "mirror") {
      const base = this.baseTraces[(this.rand() * this.baseTraces.length) | 0];
      const scale = 0.96 + this.rand() * 0.08, tScale = 0.96 + this.rand() * 0.08;
      trace = base.map(p => ({
        x: p.x * scale + (this.rand() - 0.5) * 1.6,
        y: p.y * scale + (this.rand() - 0.5) * 1.6,
        t: p.t * tScale,
      }));
    } else {
      // generative: a FRESH random human-shaped trace every swipe.
      // Noise model depends on the evasion toggle:
      //  - off: iid Gaussian (spectrally WHITE — the naive choice, caught by Δ⁴/Δ²)
      //  - on:  pink (1/f) noise + 8-12Hz physiological tremor + low-freq drift,
      //         i.e. the actual structure of human motor noise. This is the
      //         evasion a serious attacker writes once whiteness is detected.
      const r = this.rand;
      const n = 14, dur = 130 + r() * 110, len = 95 + r() * 55, arc = (r() - 0.5) * 26;
      const skew = 0.32 + r() * 0.3;
      const organic = document.getElementById("organicNoise").checked;
      // pink noise via Voss-McCartney: summed octave-rate random sources give
      // 1/f power, so successive samples are correlated instead of independent
      const octaves = 5, srcX = [], srcY = [];
      for (let k = 0; k < octaves; k++) { srcX.push(r() - 0.5); srcY.push(r() - 0.5); }
      const tremHz = 8 + r() * 4, tremPh = r() * 6.283, tremAmp = 0.55 + r() * 0.5;
      const driftX = (r() - 0.5) * 2.2, driftY = (r() - 0.5) * 2.6;
      trace = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const u = i / (n - 1);
        const p = u < skew ? (u / skew) * (u / skew) * 0.5
                           : 0.5 + (1 - Math.pow(1 - (u - skew) / (1 - skew), 2)) * 0.5;
        let nx, ny;
        if (organic) {
          // update only the octaves whose bit flipped this step (Voss)
          for (let k = 0; k < octaves; k++) {
            if (i % (1 << k) === 0) { srcX[k] = r() - 0.5; srcY[k] = r() - 0.5; }
          }
          const pinkX = srcX.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / Math.sqrt(octaves);
          const pinkY = srcY.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / Math.sqrt(octaves);
          const tSec = (u * dur) / 1000;
          const trem = Math.sin(tremPh + 6.283 * tremHz * tSec) * tremAmp;
          nx = pinkX * 2.6 + trem * 0.5 + driftX * u;
          ny = pinkY * 2.6 + trem * 0.8 + driftY * u;
        } else {
          nx = (r() - 0.5) * 2.4;
          ny = (r() - 0.5) * 2.2;
        }
        trace.push({
          x: p * len + nx,
          y: Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * arc + ny,
          t: u * dur,
        });
      }
    }
    this.swipeRun = { trace, i: 0, dir, startX, startY, t0: now };
  },
};
Bot.buildCorpus();

/* ============================ TEST BENCH ============================
   Live forensics view: every swipe is plotted and measured the moment it
   lands, distributions update per dodge, and everything of interest goes
   to the timestamped event log. Display only — the detector (AC below)
   never reads anything from here. */
const Dash = {
  seq: 0,
  t0: performance.now(),
  lastFlagSet: new Set(),
  lastBadge: "",
  selected: null,
  elLog: document.getElementById("log"),
  elGallery: document.getElementById("gallery"),

  clock() {
    const s = (performance.now() - this.t0) / 1000;
    const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
    return "T+" + String(m).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + (s % 60).toFixed(3).padStart(6, "0");
  },

  log(msg, cls) {
    const d = document.createElement("div");
    d.className = cls || "info";
    d.textContent = "[" + this.clock() + "] " + msg;
    this.elLog.appendChild(d);
    while (this.elLog.childElementCount > 600) this.elLog.removeChild(this.elLog.firstChild);
    this.elLog.scrollTop = this.elLog.scrollHeight;
  },

  reset() {
    this.elGallery.innerHTML = "";
    this.lastFlagSet = new Set();
    this.selected = null;
    document.getElementById("swipeTitle").textContent = "waiting for first swipe";
    document.getElementById("swipeMetrics").innerHTML = "";
    const c = document.getElementById("swipePlot");
    c.getContext("2d").clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    this.drawCharts();
    this.log("TELEMETRY RESET — all detector state and charts cleared", "warn");
  },

  onSwipe(f) {
    f.seq = ++this.seq;
    // nearest neighbor among the session's other swipes (shape space)
    let nn = null;
    for (const o of AC.swipes) {
      if (o === f) continue;
      const sd = AC.shapeDist(f.res, o.res);
      if (!nn || sd < nn.sd) nn = { sd, pd: AC.profileDist(f.profile, o.profile), seq: o.seq };
    }
    f.nn = nn;
    f.replayMatch = !!(nn && nn.sd < 0.015 && nn.pd < 0.30);
    this.renderSwipe(f);
    this.addThumb(f);
    const dir = f.pts[f.pts.length - 1].x >= f.pts[0].x ? "R" : "L";
    this.log(
      "SWIPE #" + f.seq + " dir=" + dir +
      " dur=" + f.dur.toFixed(0) + "ms pts=" + f.pts.length +
      " len=" + f.len.toFixed(0) + "px jitter=" + f.jitter.toFixed(2) + "px" +
      " white=" + (Number.isNaN(f.white) ? "n/a" : f.white.toFixed(2)) +
      " peakT=" + f.peakT.toFixed(2) +
      (nn ? " nnShape=" + nn.sd.toFixed(4) + "(#" + nn.seq + ")" : "") +
      (f.trusted ? "" : " [SYNTHETIC-EVENTS]") +
      (f.replayMatch ? " ** REPLAY-MATCH vs #" + nn.seq + " **" : ""),
      f.replayMatch ? "flag" : (f.trusted ? "metric" : "warn"));
  },

  renderSwipe(f) {
    this.selected = f;
    for (const el of this.elGallery.children) el.classList.toggle("sel", el.__f === f);
    document.getElementById("swipeTitle").textContent =
      "swipe #" + f.seq + (f.replayMatch ? "  ⚑ REPLAY MATCH" : "");
    const c = document.getElementById("swipePlot"), g = c.getContext("2d");
    const Wc = c.width, Hc = c.height;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, Wc, Hc);
    // grid
    g.strokeStyle = "rgba(135,146,166,0.10)";
    g.lineWidth = 1;
    for (let x = 0; x < Wc; x += 40) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, Hc); g.stroke(); }
    for (let y = 0; y < Hc; y += 40) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(Wc, y); g.stroke(); }

    const pathH = Hc - 88;
    const pts = f.pts;
    let minx = 1e9, maxx = -1e9, miny = 1e9, maxy = -1e9;
    for (const p of pts) {
      minx = Math.min(minx, p.x); maxx = Math.max(maxx, p.x);
      miny = Math.min(miny, p.y); maxy = Math.max(maxy, p.y);
    }
    const spanx = Math.max(20, maxx - minx), spany = Math.max(20, maxy - miny);
    const sc = Math.min((Wc - 70) / spanx, (pathH - 50) / spany);
    const ox = (Wc - spanx * sc) / 2 - minx * sc;
    const oy = (pathH - spany * sc) / 2 - miny * sc + 14;
    const X = p => p.x * sc + ox, Y = p => p.y * sc + oy;

    // segments colored by instantaneous speed
    let vmax = 1e-9;
    const vs = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const dt = pts[i].t - pts[i - 1].t || 1;
      const v = Math.hypot(pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x, pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y) / dt;
      vs.push(v); vmax = Math.max(vmax, v);
    }
    g.lineWidth = 3;
    g.lineCap = "round";
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const u = vs[i - 1] / vmax;
      g.strokeStyle = "hsl(" + (210 - 210 * u) + ",90%,60%)";
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(X(pts[i - 1]), Y(pts[i - 1])); g.lineTo(X(pts[i]), Y(pts[i])); g.stroke();
    }
    // sample points
    g.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)";
    for (const p of pts) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(X(p), Y(p), 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); }
    // start / end markers
    g.strokeStyle = "#3ecf8e"; g.lineWidth = 2;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(X(pts[0]), Y(pts[0]), 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke();
    g.strokeStyle = "#f4544c";
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(X(pts[pts.length - 1]), Y(pts[pts.length - 1]), 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = "#8792a6";
    g.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
    g.textAlign = "left";
    g.fillText("path (screen px) — segment color = speed (blue slow → red fast)", 10, 12);

    // velocity profile strip
    const vy0 = Hc - 66, vh = 48;
    g.fillText("velocity profile (16 samples, normalized)", 10, vy0 - 5);
    const bw = (Wc - 20) / f.profile.length;
    const pmax = Math.max(...f.profile, 1e-9);
    for (let i = 0; i < f.profile.length; i++) {
      const h = (f.profile[i] / pmax) * vh;
      g.fillStyle = "hsl(" + (210 - 210 * (f.profile[i] / pmax)) + ",90%,58%)";
      g.fillRect(10 + i * bw, vy0 + vh - h, bw - 2, h);
    }

    // metrics table
    const nn = f.nn;
    const rows = [
      ["duration", f.dur.toFixed(0) + " ms"],
      ["sample points", f.pts.length],
      ["path length", f.len.toFixed(1) + " px"],
      ["jitter (motor noise)", f.jitter.toFixed(3) + " px", f.jitter < 0.30],
      ["Δ⁴/Δ² whiteness", Number.isNaN(f.white) ? "n/a" : f.white.toFixed(2), f.white >= 2.0 && f.wamp >= 0.4],
      ["implied noise σ", f.wamp.toFixed(2) + " px"],
      ["velocity peak position", f.peakT.toFixed(3)],
      ["integer-coord fraction", (f.intFrac * 100).toFixed(0) + " %"],
      ["event provenance", f.trusted ? "trusted" : "SYNTHETIC", !f.trusted],
      ["nn shape dist", nn ? nn.sd.toFixed(4) + "  (vs #" + nn.seq + ")" : "—", f.replayMatch],
      ["nn profile dist", nn ? nn.pd.toFixed(3) : "—"],
      ["classification", f.replayMatch ? "REPLAY MATCH" : "no duplicate", f.replayMatch],
    ];
    document.getElementById("swipeMetrics").innerHTML = rows.map(r =>
      `<span class="k">${r[0]}</span><span class="v${r[2] ? " hot" : ""}">${r[1]}</span>`).join("");
  },

  addThumb(f) {
    const t = document.createElement("canvas");
    t.width = 92; t.height = 62;
    t.title = "swipe #" + f.seq;
    if (f.replayMatch) t.className = "replay";
    else if (!f.trusted) t.className = "untrusted";
    const g = t.getContext("2d");
    const pts = f.pts;
    let minx = 1e9, maxx = -1e9, miny = 1e9, maxy = -1e9;
    for (const p of pts) {
      minx = Math.min(minx, p.x); maxx = Math.max(maxx, p.x);
      miny = Math.min(miny, p.y); maxy = Math.max(maxy, p.y);
    }
    const sc = Math.min(76 / Math.max(20, maxx - minx), 40 / Math.max(20, maxy - miny));
    const ox = (92 - (maxx - minx) * sc) / 2 - minx * sc;
    const oy = (54 - (maxy - miny) * sc) / 2 - miny * sc;
    g.strokeStyle = f.replayMatch ? "#f4544c" : f.trusted ? "#7ec9ff" : "#f5b942";
    g.lineWidth = 1.6;
    g.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const x = pts[i].x * sc + ox, y = pts[i].y * sc + oy;
      i ? g.lineTo(x, y) : g.moveTo(x, y);
    }
    g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = "#8792a6";
    g.font = "9px ui-monospace, monospace";
    g.fillText("#" + f.seq + " " + f.dur.toFixed(0) + "ms", 4, 58);
    t.__f = f;
    t.onclick = () => this.renderSwipe(f);
    this.elGallery.appendChild(t);
    while (this.elGallery.childElementCount > 24) this.elGallery.removeChild(this.elGallery.firstChild);
  },

  onDodge(rt, margin) {
    this.drawCharts();
    this.log("dodge: RT " + rt.toFixed(0) + "ms · margin " + margin.toFixed(2) + "s · game speed " + game.speed.toFixed(1), "metric");
  },

  hist(canvasId, values, lo, hi, bins, unit, statsId, statsFn) {
    const c = document.getElementById(canvasId), g = c.getContext("2d");
    g.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    g.strokeStyle = "rgba(135,146,166,0.12)";
    for (let y = 0; y < c.height; y += 30) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(c.width, y); g.stroke(); }
    g.fillStyle = "#8792a6";
    g.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
    g.textAlign = "left"; g.fillText(lo + unit, 4, c.height - 4);
    g.textAlign = "right"; g.fillText(hi + unit, c.width - 4, c.height - 4);
    if (!values.length) {
      g.textAlign = "center"; g.fillText("no samples yet", c.width / 2, c.height / 2);
      document.getElementById(statsId).textContent = "n 0";
      return;
    }
    const counts = new Array(bins).fill(0);
    for (const v of values) {
      const b = Math.max(0, Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor(((v - lo) / (hi - lo)) * bins)));
      counts[b]++;
    }
    const cmax = Math.max(...counts);
    const bw = (c.width - 8) / bins;
    for (let i = 0; i < bins; i++) {
      const h = (counts[i] / cmax) * (c.height - 34);
      g.fillStyle = "#4da3ff";
      g.fillRect(4 + i * bw, c.height - 16 - h, bw - 1.5, h);
    }
    document.getElementById(statsId).textContent = statsFn(values);
  },

  drawCharts() {
    this.hist("rtChart", AC.rts, 0, 800, 28, "ms", "rtStats", v => {
      const st = AC.stats(v);
      const sk = v.length >= 8 ? AC.skewness(v) : NaN;
      return "n " + v.length + " · mean " + st.mean.toFixed(0) + "ms · sd " + st.sd.toFixed(0) +
        " · cv " + st.cv.toFixed(2) + " · min " + st.min.toFixed(0) +
        (Number.isNaN(sk) ? "" : " · skew " + sk.toFixed(2) + (sk < 0.15 ? " (no lapse tail)" : ""));
    });
    this.hist("mChart", AC.margins, 0, 1.2, 24, "s", "mStats", v => {
      const st = AC.stats(v);
      return "n " + v.length + " · mean " + st.mean.toFixed(2) + "s · cv " + st.cv.toFixed(2) +
        (st.cv < 0.22 && v.length >= 8 ? " (metronome-consistent)" : "");
    });
  },
};

/* =========================== ANTI-CHEAT ===========================
   Pure behavioral analysis over the input/telemetry stream. It has no
   knowledge of Bot.mode. Signals:
   1. reaction   — dodge RT distribution (floor, variance, lapse-tail shape)
   2. kinematics — swipe micro-motion (motor noise, velocity-profile diversity)
   3. noise character — spectral whiteness of the micro-motion (injected vs motor)
   4. replay     — near-duplicate swipes in shape AND timing
   5. perfection — dodge margins + survival humans don't produce
   6. behavior texture — aborts, contested-space entries, cashout patterns
   7. integrity  — synthetic event provenance (isTrusted), coordinate granularity */
const AC = {
  swipes: [], rts: [], margins: [],
  deaths: 0, dodges: 0, moves: 0, rowsPassed: 0,
  aborts: 0,
  risks: [],
  pendingRisks: [],
  runEnds: [],
  untrustedSeen: false, keyboardOnly: true,

  recordSwipe(s) {
    this.keyboardOnly = false;
    if (!s.trusted) this.untrustedSeen = true;
    if (s.points.length >= 5 && s.dur > 30) {
      const f = this.featurize(s);
      this.swipes.push(f);
      if (this.swipes.length > 80) this.swipes.shift();
      Dash.onSwipe(f);
    }
  },
  recordKey(trusted) { if (!trusted) this.untrustedSeen = true; },
  recordDodge(rt, margin) {
    this.dodges++;
    if (rt > 40 && rt < 2000) this.rts.push(rt);
    if (margin > 0) this.margins.push(margin);
    if (this.rts.length > 120) this.rts.shift();
    if (this.margins.length > 120) this.margins.shift();
    Dash.onDodge(rt, margin);
  },
  recordAbort() {
    this.aborts++;
    Dash.log("gesture aborted — touch released with no lane change committed (" + this.aborts + " total)", "info");
  },
  recordRisk() {
    this.pendingRisks.push({ at: performance.now() });
    Dash.log("CONTESTED move — entered a lane with live traffic inside the threat window", "warn");
  },
  recordRunEnd(kind) {
    this.runEnds.push(kind);
    if (kind === "crash") {
      Dash.log("RUN END: CRASH at score " + game.score + " — payout $" + (ENTRY_FEE * multiplier()).toFixed(2) + " forfeited", "flag");
    } else {
      Dash.log("RUN END: CASHOUT — banked $" + game.banked.toFixed(2) + " at " + multiplier().toFixed(2) + "x (score " + game.score + ")", "good");
    }
  },
  recordDeath() {
    this.deaths++;
    const now = performance.now();
    for (const r of this.pendingRisks) {
      const fatal = now - r.at <= 1400;
      this.risks.push(!fatal);
      Dash.log("contested move resolved: " + (fatal ? "FATAL (crash within 1.4s)" : "survived"), fatal ? "warn" : "info");
    }
    this.pendingRisks = [];
  },
  tickRisks(now) {
    while (this.pendingRisks.length && now - this.pendingRisks[0].at > 1400) {
      this.pendingRisks.shift();
      this.risks.push(true);
      Dash.log("contested move resolved: survived (" + this.risks.filter(Boolean).length + "/" + this.risks.length + " survive rate)", "info");
    }
  },

  reset() {
    this.swipes = []; this.rts = []; this.margins = [];
    this.deaths = 0; this.dodges = 0; this.moves = 0; this.rowsPassed = 0;
    this.aborts = 0; this.risks = []; this.pendingRisks = []; this.runEnds = [];
    this.untrustedSeen = false; this.keyboardOnly = true;
    game.deaths = 0; game.score = 0;
  },

  featurize(s) {
    const pts = s.points;
    const res = this.resample(pts, 16);
    let jitter = 0, jn = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
      const mx = (pts[i - 1].x + pts[i + 1].x) / 2, my = (pts[i - 1].y + pts[i + 1].y) / 2;
      jitter += Math.hypot(pts[i].x - mx, pts[i].y - my); jn++;
    }
    jitter = jn ? jitter / jn : 0;
    let peakV = 0, peakT = 0.5;
    const t0 = pts[0].t, dur = pts[pts.length - 1].t - t0 || 1;
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const dt = pts[i].t - pts[i - 1].t || 1;
      const v = Math.hypot(pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x, pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y) / dt;
      if (v > peakV) { peakV = v; peakT = (pts[i].t - t0) / dur; }
    }
    let ints = 0;
    for (const p of pts) if (p.x === Math.round(p.x) && p.y === Math.round(p.y)) ints++;
    const profile = this.speedProfile(pts, 16);
    // High-frequency noise energy: the 4th difference annihilates smooth motion
    // (through cubic) but amplifies iid noise by sqrt(70)σ; the 2nd difference
    // carries curvature. Their sd ratio separates injected white noise (~3.4 for
    // pure noise, ~2.2 riding a curve) from band-limited human motion.
    let white = NaN, wamp = 0;
    if (pts.length >= 7) {
      const dif = a => a.slice(1).map((v, i) => v - a[i]);
      const sdv = a => {
        const m = a.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0) / a.length;
        return Math.sqrt(a.reduce((x, y) => x + (y - m) * (y - m), 0) / a.length);
      };
      const xs = pts.map(p => p.x), ys = pts.map(p => p.y);
      const d2x = dif(dif(xs)), d2y = dif(dif(ys));
      const d4x = dif(dif(d2x)), d4y = dif(dif(d2y));
      const e4 = sdv(d4x) + sdv(d4y), e2 = sdv(d2x) + sdv(d2y);
      white = e4 / (e2 + 1e-9);
      wamp = e4 / (2 * Math.sqrt(70)); // implied per-point noise sigma in px
    }
    let len = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) len += Math.hypot(pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x, pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y);
    return { res, profile, white, wamp, jitter, peakT, len, pts,
             dur: s.dur, trusted: s.trusted, intFrac: ints / pts.length };
  },

  speedProfile(pts, n) {
    const t0 = pts[0].t, dur = pts[pts.length - 1].t - t0 || 1;
    const v = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const dt = pts[i].t - pts[i - 1].t || 1;
      v.push({ t: (pts[i].t - t0) / dur, s: Math.hypot(pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x, pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y) / dt });
    }
    const out = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const target = k / (n - 1);
      let i = 0;
      while (i < v.length - 1 && v[i].t < target) i++;
      out.push(v[i].s);
    }
    const mean = out.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n || 1;
    return out.map(x => x / mean);
  },

  profileDist(a, b) {
    let s = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += Math.abs(a[i] - b[i]);
    return s / a.length;
  },

  resample(pts, n) {
    const d = [0];
    for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++)
      d.push(d[i - 1] + Math.hypot(pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x, pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y));
    const total = d[d.length - 1] || 1;
    const out = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const target = (k / (n - 1)) * total;
      let i = 1;
      while (i < d.length - 1 && d[i] < target) i++;
      const span = d[i] - d[i - 1] || 1;
      const u = (target - d[i - 1]) / span;
      out.push({
        x: (pts[i - 1].x + (pts[i].x - pts[i - 1].x) * u - pts[0].x) / total,
        y: (pts[i - 1].y + (pts[i].y - pts[i - 1].y) * u - pts[0].y) / total,
      });
    }
    if (out[n - 1].x < 0) for (const p of out) p.x = -p.x;
    return out;
  },

  shapeDist(a, b) {
    let s = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += Math.hypot(a[i].x - b[i].x, a[i].y - b[i].y);
    return s / a.length;
  },

  stats(arr) {
    const n = arr.length;
    const mean = arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
    const sd = Math.sqrt(arr.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) * (b - mean), 0) / n);
    return { mean, sd, min: Math.min(...arr), cv: mean ? sd / mean : 0 };
  },

  skewness(arr) {
    const n = arr.length;
    const mean = arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
    let m2 = 0, m3 = 0;
    for (const x of arr) { const d = x - mean; m2 += d * d; m3 += d * d * d; }
    m2 /= n; m3 /= n;
    return m2 > 1e-9 ? m3 / Math.pow(m2, 1.5) : 0;
  },

  analyze() {
    const signals = [];
    const flags = [];

    // 1. reaction time — floor, variance, and distribution SHAPE. Human RT is
    // ex-Gaussian: a heavy right tail of attentional lapses. Sampling a clean
    // Gaussian produces a symmetric distribution no human produces.
    {
      const ready = this.rts.length >= 6;
      let sus = 0, detail = "need " + Math.max(0, 6 - this.rts.length) + " more dodges";
      if (ready) {
        const st = this.stats(this.rts);
        sus = 0;
        if (st.min < 130) { sus += 0.55; flags.push("superhuman reaction floor: " + st.min.toFixed(0) + "ms"); }
        if (st.cv < 0.10) { sus += 0.5; flags.push("reaction variance impossibly tight (cv " + st.cv.toFixed(2) + ")"); }
        else if (st.cv < 0.17) sus += 0.25;
        if (st.mean < 165) sus += 0.3;
        let skewTxt = "";
        if (this.rts.length >= 20) {
          const sk = this.skewness(this.rts);
          skewTxt = " · skew " + sk.toFixed(2);
          if (sk < 0.15) { sus += 0.3; flags.push("no attentional lapses: RT distribution symmetric (skew " + sk.toFixed(2) + ")"); }
        }
        sus = Math.min(1, sus);
        detail = "mean " + st.mean.toFixed(0) + "ms · min " + st.min.toFixed(0) + " · cv " + st.cv.toFixed(2) + skewTxt;
      }
      signals.push({ name: "reaction time", sus, ready, detail });
    }

    // 2. swipe kinematics — motor-noise magnitude + velocity-profile diversity
    {
      const sw = this.swipes;
      const ready = sw.length >= 6;
      let sus = 0, detail = this.keyboardOnly ? "keyboard play — n/a" : "need " + Math.max(0, 6 - sw.length) + " more swipes";
      if (ready) {
        const jit = this.stats(sw.map(s => s.jitter));
        const peaks = this.stats(sw.map(s => s.peakT));
        sus = 0;
        if (jit.mean < 0.30) { sus += 0.6; flags.push("swipes have no motor noise (jitter " + jit.mean.toFixed(2) + "px)"); }
        else if (jit.mean < 0.55) sus += 0.25;
        if (peaks.sd < 0.035) { sus += 0.35; flags.push("velocity profiles machine-uniform"); }
        sus = Math.min(1, sus);
        detail = "jitter " + jit.mean.toFixed(2) + "px · peak-pos sd " + peaks.sd.toFixed(3);
      }
      signals.push({ name: "swipe kinematics", sus, ready: ready && !this.keyboardOnly, detail });
    }

    // 3. noise character — injected iid noise is spectrally white; human motor
    // noise is band-limited. Catches bots that add randomness to defeat the
    // jitter check. Threshold is an uncalibrated prior until a human corpus
    // pins the real distribution.
    {
      const ws = this.swipes.filter(s => !Number.isNaN(s.white));
      const ready = ws.length >= 8 && !this.keyboardOnly;
      let sus = 0, detail = this.keyboardOnly ? "keyboard play — n/a" : "need " + Math.max(0, 8 - ws.length) + " more swipes";
      if (ready) {
        const ratio = ws.reduce((a, s) => a + s.white, 0) / ws.length;
        const amp = ws.reduce((a, s) => a + s.wamp, 0) / ws.length;
        sus = 0;
        if (ratio >= 2.0 && amp >= 0.4) {
          sus = 0.7;
          flags.push("high-frequency noise energy is white-noise-shaped (ratio " + ratio.toFixed(2) + ") — injected, not motor [uncalibrated prior]");
        } else if (ratio >= 1.6 && amp >= 0.4) sus = 0.3;
        detail = "Δ⁴/Δ² energy " + ratio.toFixed(2) + " · implied noise σ " + amp.toFixed(2) + "px (white ≥ ~2.0)";
      }
      signals.push({ name: "noise character", sus, ready, detail });
    }

    // 4. replay similarity — near-duplicates must match in SHAPE and TIMING.
    // Perturbed replays of a recorded corpus match both; humans vary pacing
    // even when shapes converge.
    {
      const sw = this.swipes.slice(-40);
      const ready = sw.length >= 8;
      let sus = 0, detail = this.keyboardOnly ? "keyboard play — n/a" : "need " + Math.max(0, 8 - sw.length) + " more swipes";
      if (ready) {
        let close = 0, pairs = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < sw.length; i++)
          for (let j = i + 1; j < sw.length; j++) {
            pairs++;
            if (this.shapeDist(sw[i].res, sw[j].res) < 0.015 &&
                this.profileDist(sw[i].profile, sw[j].profile) < 0.30) close++;
          }
        const frac = pairs ? close / pairs : 0;
        sus = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (frac - 0.05) / 0.25));
        if (frac > 0.25) flags.push("swipes repeat in shape AND timing: " + (frac * 100).toFixed(0) + "% near-duplicate pairs (trace replay)");
        detail = (frac * 100).toFixed(0) + "% shape+timing near-duplicate pairs across " + sw.length + " swipes";
      }
      signals.push({ name: "replay similarity", sus, ready: ready && !this.keyboardOnly, detail });
    }

    // 5. perfection — dodge-margin consistency and survival humans don't produce
    {
      const ready = this.dodges >= 12;
      let sus = 0, detail = "need " + Math.max(0, 12 - this.dodges) + " more dodges";
      if (ready) {
        sus = 0;
        if (this.margins.length >= 8) {
          const m = this.stats(this.margins);
          if (m.cv < 0.22) { sus += 0.45; flags.push("dodge margins metronome-consistent (cv " + m.cv.toFixed(2) + ")"); }
          detail = "margin cv " + m.cv.toFixed(2) + " · ";
        } else detail = "";
        const dodgesPerDeath = this.dodges / Math.max(1, this.deaths);
        if (this.deaths === 0 && this.dodges >= 40) { sus += 0.5; flags.push("zero deaths across " + this.dodges + " dodges"); }
        else if (dodgesPerDeath > 60) sus += 0.3;
        sus = Math.min(1, sus);
        detail += this.dodges + " dodges · " + this.deaths + " deaths";
      }
      signals.push({ name: "perfection", sus, ready, detail });
    }

    // 6. behavior texture — session-level habits automation doesn't reproduce:
    // humans abort gestures, wander into contested space (and pay for it),
    // and actually bank runs. Lookahead-verified play never risks anything,
    // and when a bot injects "errors" they are always survivable.
    {
      const ready = this.moves >= 14;
      let sus = 0, detail = "need " + Math.max(0, 14 - this.moves) + " more moves";
      if (ready) {
        sus = 0;
        const parts = [];
        if (this.aborts === 0 && this.moves >= 14) { sus += 0.25; parts.push("0 aborted gestures"); }
        else parts.push(this.aborts + " aborted gestures");
        if (this.risks.length === 0 && this.moves >= 14) {
          sus += 0.45;
          flags.push("never enters contested space across " + this.moves + " moves (lookahead-verified play)");
          parts.push("0 contested moves");
        } else if (this.risks.length >= 3) {
          const survived = this.risks.filter(Boolean).length;
          if (survived === this.risks.length) {
            sus += this.risks.length >= 5 ? 0.6 : 0.45;
            flags.push("risky moves never cost anything: " + survived + "/" + this.risks.length + " contested moves survived");
          }
          parts.push(survived + "/" + this.risks.length + " contested survived");
        } else parts.push(this.risks.length + " contested moves");
        if (this.runEnds.length >= 3 && !this.runEnds.includes("cashout")) {
          sus += 0.3;
          flags.push("never banks a run: " + this.runEnds.length + " runs, 0 cash-outs (farm pattern)");
        }
        sus = Math.min(1, sus);
        detail = parts.join(" · ") + " · " + this.runEnds.length + " runs ended";
      }
      signals.push({ name: "behavior texture", sus, ready, detail });
    }

    // 7. event integrity — provenance. Cheap to spoof at the OS level, so it is
    // corroborating evidence, never the case.
    {
      const sw = this.swipes;
      const ready = sw.length >= 3 || this.untrustedSeen;
      let sus = 0, detail = "collecting";
      if (ready) {
        sus = 0;
        if (this.untrustedSeen) { sus += 0.8; flags.push("synthetic input events (isTrusted=false)"); }
        if (sw.length >= 5) {
          const intFrac = sw.reduce((a, s) => a + s.intFrac, 0) / sw.length;
          detail = this.untrustedSeen ? "synthetic events observed" : "events trusted · int-coord " + (intFrac * 100).toFixed(0) + "%";
        } else detail = this.untrustedSeen ? "synthetic events observed" : "events trusted";
        sus = Math.min(1, sus);
      }
      signals.push({ name: "event integrity", sus, ready, detail });
    }

    const weights = {
      "reaction time": 0.19, "swipe kinematics": 0.14, "noise character": 0.13,
      "replay similarity": 0.19, "perfection": 0.12, "behavior texture": 0.13,
      "event integrity": 0.10,
    };
    let wsum = 0, acc = 0, readyCount = 0;
    for (const s of signals) {
      if (!s.ready) continue;
      readyCount++;
      wsum += weights[s.name];
      acc += weights[s.name] * s.sus;
    }
    let overall = wsum ? acc / wsum : 0;
    // Escalation: an ensemble average must not wash out convergent evidence.
    // One smoking gun, or several independently suspicious signals, escalates.
    let maxSus = 0, hot = 0;
    for (const s of signals) {
      if (!s.ready) continue;
      if (s.sus > maxSus) maxSus = s.sus;
      if (s.sus >= 0.55) hot++;
    }
    // Tiered enforcement: ONE strong signal is enough to flag for review
    // (SUSPECT), but acting on an account (BOT) wants convergent evidence from
    // independent signals — that is what keeps false-positive bans near zero.
    if (maxSus >= 0.68) overall = Math.max(overall, 0.50);
    if (maxSus >= 0.85) overall = Math.max(overall, maxSus * 0.75);
    if (hot >= 2) overall = Math.max(overall, 0.62);
    if (hot >= 3) overall = Math.max(overall, 0.72);
    return { signals, flags, overall, ready: readyCount >= 2 };
  },
};

/* ==================== SERVER-SIDE ACCOUNT ANALYTICS ====================
   These two models are OFFLINE analysis over account history, not live-session
   forensics. They exist because the swipe-level signals above are defeatable
   by an attacker who models human motor noise properly (see the organic-noise
   evasion), while these are not: the economy binds by arithmetic, and session
   scheduling is expensive for a farm to fake without giving up throughput. */
const Econ = {
  ENTRY: 5,
  rake: 0.20,
  // head-to-head: pot = 2*entry, house takes rake, winner takes the rest
  winNet() { return 2 * this.ENTRY * (1 - this.rake) - this.ENTRY; },
  loseNet() { return -this.ENTRY; },
  evAt(p) { return p * this.winNet() + (1 - p) * this.loseNet(); },
  breakEven() { return -this.loseNet() / (this.winNet() - this.loseNet()); },

  sim(nPlayers, nGames, botWR) {
    const r = mulberry32(20260822);
    // latent skill ~ N(0,1); match random pairs; logistic win probability.
    // Win rate then EMERGES from play rather than being assigned.
    const skill = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nPlayers; i++) skill.push(gauss(r, 0, 1));
    // SKILL-MATCHED pairing, the way a real platform seeds head-to-head games:
    // opponents come from a narrow band of the sorted skill ladder. This is what
    // compresses observed win rates toward 50% and makes a sustained high win
    // rate such a loud outlier.
    const order = skill.map((s, i) => i).sort((a, b) => skill[a] - skill[b]);
    const band = Math.max(2, Math.round(nPlayers * 0.06));
    const wins = new Array(nPlayers).fill(0), played = new Array(nPlayers).fill(0);
    const K = 1.15;
    for (let g = 0; g < nGames * nPlayers / 2; g++) {
      const ia = (r() * nPlayers) | 0;
      let ib = ia + (((r() * (2 * band + 1)) | 0) - band);
      ib = Math.max(0, Math.min(nPlayers - 1, ib));
      if (ib === ia) ib = (ia + 1) % nPlayers;
      const a = order[ia], b = order[ib];
      const pa = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-K * (skill[a] - skill[b])));
      const aWins = r() < pa;
      played[a]++; played[b]++;
      if (aWins) wins[a]++; else wins[b]++;
    }
    const rates = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nPlayers; i++) if (played[i] >= 30) rates.push(wins[i] / played[i]);
    const mean = rates.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0) / rates.length;
    const sd = Math.sqrt(rates.reduce((x, y) => x + (y - mean) * (y - mean), 0) / rates.length);
    const z = (botWR - mean) / sd;
    const above = rates.filter(v => v >= botWR).length;
    // how much of the observed spread is just binomial sampling noise vs real
    // skill: with nGames games the noise floor is sqrt(p(1-p)/n)
    const noise = Math.sqrt(0.25 / nGames);
    return { rates, mean, sd, z, noise, nGames, botWR,
             pctile: 100 * (1 - above / rates.length) };
  },

  draw() {
    const nP = +document.getElementById("popN").value;
    const nG = +document.getElementById("popG").value;
    const botWR = +document.getElementById("botWR").value / 100;
    this.rake = +document.getElementById("rakePct").value / 100;
    document.getElementById("botWRLbl").textContent = (botWR * 100).toFixed(0) + "%";
    document.getElementById("popNLbl").textContent = nP;
    document.getElementById("popGLbl").textContent = nG;
    document.getElementById("rakeLbl").textContent = (this.rake * 100).toFixed(0);
    document.getElementById("rakeLbl2").textContent = (this.rake * 100).toFixed(0) + "%";
    document.getElementById("feeLbl").textContent = this.ENTRY;

    const s = this.sim(nP, nG, botWR);
    const be = this.breakEven();

    // ---- population histogram ----
    const c = document.getElementById("popChart"), g = c.getContext("2d");
    const Wc = c.width, Hc = c.height;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, Wc, Hc);
    const lo = 0.25, hi = 0.95, bins = 56;
    const counts = new Array(bins).fill(0);
    for (const v of s.rates) {
      const b = Math.max(0, Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor(((v - lo) / (hi - lo)) * bins)));
      counts[b]++;
    }
    const cmax = Math.max(...counts, 1);
    const bw = (Wc - 16) / bins;
    const xOf = v => 8 + ((v - lo) / (hi - lo)) * (Wc - 16);
    for (let i = 0; i < bins; i++) {
      const h = (counts[i] / cmax) * (Hc - 44);
      const v = lo + (i + 0.5) / bins * (hi - lo);
      g.fillStyle = v >= be ? "rgba(244,84,76,0.75)" : "#4da3ff";
      g.fillRect(8 + i * bw, Hc - 26 - h, bw - 1, h);
    }
    // rake wall
    g.strokeStyle = "#f5b942"; g.lineWidth = 2; g.setLineDash([4, 3]);
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(xOf(be), 12); g.lineTo(xOf(be), Hc - 26); g.stroke();
    g.setLineDash([]);
    g.fillStyle = "#f5b942"; g.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace"; g.textAlign = "center";
    g.fillText("rake wall " + (be * 100).toFixed(1) + "%", xOf(be), 10);
    // bot marker
    g.strokeStyle = "#f4544c"; g.lineWidth = 2;
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(xOf(botWR), 12); g.lineTo(xOf(botWR), Hc - 26); g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = "#f4544c";
    g.fillText("BOT " + (botWR * 100).toFixed(0) + "%", xOf(botWR), Hc - 14);
    g.fillStyle = "#8792a6"; g.textAlign = "left";
    g.fillText((lo * 100).toFixed(0) + "%", 8, Hc - 4);
    g.textAlign = "right"; g.fillText((hi * 100).toFixed(0) + "%", Wc - 8, Hc - 4);
    g.textAlign = "left";
    g.fillText("player win-rate distribution (n=" + s.rates.length + ", " + nG + " games each)", 8, Hc - 32 - (Hc - 44));

    document.getElementById("popStats").textContent =
      "skill-matched population: mean " + (s.mean * 100).toFixed(1) + "% · sd " + (s.sd * 100).toFixed(1) +
      "pp (binomial noise floor " + (s.noise * 100).toFixed(1) + "pp) · bot z-score " + s.z.toFixed(1) +
      "σ · bot at the " + s.pctile.toFixed(2) + "th percentile";

    // ---- bankroll curves ----
    const c2 = document.getElementById("bankChart"), g2 = c2.getContext("2d");
    const W2 = c2.width, H2 = c2.height;
    g2.clearRect(0, 0, W2, H2);
    const series = [
      { p: botWR, col: "#f4544c", label: "bot " + (botWR * 100).toFixed(0) + "%" },
      { p: be, col: "#f5b942", label: "break-even" },
      { p: s.mean, col: "#4da3ff", label: "median human" },
    ];
    const N = nG;
    let ymin = 0, ymax = 0;
    for (const ser of series) {
      const end = this.evAt(ser.p) * N;
      ymin = Math.min(ymin, end); ymax = Math.max(ymax, end);
    }
    const pad = Math.max(10, (ymax - ymin) * 0.1);
    ymin -= pad; ymax += pad;
    const yOf = v => H2 - 24 - ((v - ymin) / (ymax - ymin)) * (H2 - 40);
    g2.strokeStyle = "rgba(135,146,166,0.25)"; g2.lineWidth = 1;
    g2.beginPath(); g2.moveTo(8, yOf(0)); g2.lineTo(W2 - 8, yOf(0)); g2.stroke();
    for (const ser of series) {
      g2.strokeStyle = ser.col; g2.lineWidth = 2;
      g2.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i <= 60; i++) {
        const n = (i / 60) * N;
        const x = 8 + (i / 60) * (W2 - 16);
        i ? g2.lineTo(x, yOf(this.evAt(ser.p) * n)) : g2.moveTo(x, yOf(this.evAt(ser.p) * n));
      }
      g2.stroke();
      g2.fillStyle = ser.col; g2.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace"; g2.textAlign = "right";
      g2.fillText(ser.label + "  $" + (this.evAt(ser.p) * N).toFixed(0), W2 - 10, yOf(this.evAt(ser.p) * N) - 4);
    }
    g2.fillStyle = "#8792a6"; g2.textAlign = "left";
    g2.fillText("expected bankroll over " + N + " games ($" + this.ENTRY + " entry)", 8, 12);

    document.getElementById("bankStats").textContent =
      "EV/game: bot " + (this.evAt(botWR) >= 0 ? "+" : "") + "$" + this.evAt(botWR).toFixed(2) +
      " · median human $" + this.evAt(s.mean).toFixed(2) +
      " · house take $" + (2 * this.ENTRY * this.rake).toFixed(2) + "/game";

    // ---- the table ----
    const profitable = botWR > be;
    const rows = [
      ["entry / pot", "$" + this.ENTRY + " / $" + (2 * this.ENTRY)],
      ["rake", (this.rake * 100).toFixed(0) + "%  ($" + (2 * this.ENTRY * this.rake).toFixed(2) + ")"],
      ["winner nets", "+$" + this.winNet().toFixed(2)],
      ["loser nets", "-$" + this.ENTRY.toFixed(2)],
      ["break-even win rate", (be * 100).toFixed(1) + " %"],
      ["bot win rate", (botWR * 100).toFixed(0) + " %", profitable],
      ["bot EV / game", (this.evAt(botWR) >= 0 ? "+" : "") + "$" + this.evAt(botWR).toFixed(2), profitable],
      ["bot z-score", s.z.toFixed(1) + " σ", s.z > 4],
      ["percentile", s.pctile.toFixed(2) + " th", s.z > 4],
      ["verdict", profitable && s.z > 4 ? "PROFITABLE → DETECTABLE" : profitable ? "profitable, low signal" : "undetectable, unprofitable",
        profitable && s.z > 4],
    ];
    document.getElementById("econTable").innerHTML = rows.map(r =>
      `<span class="k">${r[0]}</span><span class="v${r[2] ? " hot" : ""}">${r[1]}</span>`).join("");
  },
};

const Cadence = {
  // circadian activity weight by hour (0-23): humans sleep, farms do not
  curve: [.05,.02,.01,.01,.01,.02,.06,.18,.35,.45,.5,.55,.6,.58,.55,.6,.7,.85,1,1,.95,.8,.5,.2],

  sim() {
    const r = mulberry32(77003);
    const DAYS = 7, out = { human: [], farm: [], sched: [] };
    // HUMAN: session bursts placed by circadian weight; log-normal gaps inside
    for (let d = 0; d < DAYS; d++) {
      const sessions = 2 + ((r() * 3) | 0);
      for (let s = 0; s < sessions; s++) {
        let hr;
        do { hr = r() * 24; } while (r() > this.curve[hr | 0]);
        let t = d * 86400 + hr * 3600;
        const games = 3 + ((r() * 12) | 0);
        for (let gme = 0; gme < games; gme++) {
          out.human.push(t);
          t += Math.exp(gauss(r, Math.log(38), 0.55)); // gap between games
        }
      }
    }
    // NAIVE FARM: fixed cadence, 24/7, tiny jitter
    for (let t = 0; t < DAYS * 86400; t += 45 + (r() - 0.5) * 4) out.farm.push(t);
    // SCHEDULED BOT: mimics circadian + log-normal gaps + sleep block
    for (let d = 0; d < DAYS; d++) {
      const sessions = 3 + ((r() * 3) | 0);
      for (let s = 0; s < sessions; s++) {
        let hr;
        do { hr = r() * 24; } while (r() > this.curve[hr | 0]);
        let t = d * 86400 + hr * 3600;
        const games = 6 + ((r() * 18) | 0);
        for (let gme = 0; gme < games; gme++) {
          out.sched.push(t);
          t += Math.exp(gauss(r, Math.log(34), 0.5));
        }
      }
    }
    for (const k in out) out[k].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    return out;
  },

  metrics(times) {
    const gaps = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < times.length; i++) gaps.push(times[i] - times[i - 1]);
    const inSess = gaps.filter(g => g < 300);
    const mean = inSess.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (inSess.length || 1);
    const sd = Math.sqrt(inSess.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) * (b - mean), 0) / (inSess.length || 1));
    const hours = new Array(24).fill(0);
    for (const t of times) hours[Math.floor(t / 3600) % 24]++;
    const active = hours.filter(h => h > 0).length;
    const longestIdle = Math.max(...gaps) / 3600;
    return { n: times.length, cv: mean ? sd / mean : 0, activeHours: active, longestIdle, hours, gaps };
  },

  draw() {
    const s = this.sim();
    const m = { human: this.metrics(s.human), farm: this.metrics(s.farm), sched: this.metrics(s.sched) };

    // hour-of-day profile
    const c = document.getElementById("cadChart"), g = c.getContext("2d");
    g.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    const cols = [["human", "#3ecf8e"], ["farm", "#f4544c"], ["sched", "#f5b942"]];
    let hmax = 0;
    for (const [k] of cols) hmax = Math.max(hmax, ...m[k].hours.map(v => v / m[k].n));
    const bw = (c.width - 20) / 24;
    for (let h = 0; h < 24; h++) {
      cols.forEach(([k, col], i) => {
        const v = (m[k].hours[h] / m[k].n) / hmax;
        const hh = v * (c.height - 40);
        g.fillStyle = col;
        g.globalAlpha = 0.85;
        g.fillRect(10 + h * bw + i * (bw / 3), c.height - 22 - hh, bw / 3 - 1, hh);
      });
    }
    g.globalAlpha = 1;
    g.fillStyle = "#8792a6"; g.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace"; g.textAlign = "left";
    g.fillText("00", 10, c.height - 8); g.textAlign = "center";
    g.fillText("12", c.width / 2, c.height - 8); g.textAlign = "right";
    g.fillText("23", c.width - 10, c.height - 8);
    g.textAlign = "left";
    g.fillText("share of games played, by hour of day", 10, 12);

    // gap histogram (log scale)
    const c2 = document.getElementById("gapChart"), g2 = c2.getContext("2d");
    g2.clearRect(0, 0, c2.width, c2.height);
    const bins = 40, lo = Math.log(5), hi = Math.log(3600);
    cols.forEach(([k, col]) => {
      const counts = new Array(bins).fill(0);
      const gs = m[k].gaps.filter(v => v > 4 && v < 3600);
      for (const v of gs) {
        const b = Math.max(0, Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor(((Math.log(v) - lo) / (hi - lo)) * bins)));
        counts[b]++;
      }
      const cm = Math.max(...counts, 1);
      g2.strokeStyle = col; g2.lineWidth = 2; g2.beginPath();
      for (let i = 0; i < bins; i++) {
        const x = 8 + (i / (bins - 1)) * (c2.width - 16);
        const y = c2.height - 22 - (counts[i] / cm) * (c2.height - 40);
        i ? g2.lineTo(x, y) : g2.moveTo(x, y);
      }
      g2.stroke();
    });
    g2.fillStyle = "#8792a6"; g2.font = "10px ui-monospace, monospace";
    g2.textAlign = "left"; g2.fillText("5s", 8, c2.height - 8);
    g2.textAlign = "right"; g2.fillText("1h", c2.width - 8, c2.height - 8);
    g2.textAlign = "left"; g2.fillText("in-session gap distribution", 8, 12);

    const row = (k, label) => {
      const x = m[k];
      return [
        [label + " games/wk", x.n],
        [label + " gap cv", x.cv.toFixed(2), x.cv < 0.15],
        [label + " active hours", x.activeHours + "/24", x.activeHours >= 23],
        [label + " longest idle", x.longestIdle.toFixed(1) + " h", x.longestIdle < 3],
      ];
    };
    const rows = [...row("human", "human"), ...row("farm", "farm"), ...row("sched", "sched")];
    rows.push(["farm verdict", "CAUGHT (no sleep, rigid cadence)", true]);
    rows.push(["scheduled verdict", "passes cadence checks", false]);
